## 將 IFC 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　619f58f00142de75bf-58

**english**　Import IFC To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-ifc-to-indoor-dataset.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-ifc-to-indoor-dataset.html

**text**　import arcpy

#Set the IFC file to import
in_bim_file_workspace= 'C:/Indoors/AdminBuilding.ifc'

#Set target layers that conform to the ArcGIS Indoors Information model and Facility Name
target_facility_features='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Facilities'
facility_name="Administrative Building"
target_level_features='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Levels'
target_unit_features='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Units'
target_detail_features='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Details'
target_unit3d_features='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor3D/Units3D'
target_detail3d_features='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor3D/Details3D'
target_facilities3d_features='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor3D/Facilities3D'

#Set which floor is the ground floor, and what elevation method to use
ground_floor_name="0"
ground_elevation_zero = 'SET_GROUND_ELEVATION_ZERO'

#Map additional unit attributes from spaces features
unit_properties_mapping='NAME_LONG "Long Name" true true false 255 Text 0 0,First,#,"Spaces",Family,0,255;' \
                            'CUSTOM_FIELD_DEPARTMENT "Department" true true false 255 Text 0 0,First,#,"Spaces",Department,0,255;' \
                            'CUSTOM_FIELD_FILEUPDATE "IFC File Update" true true false 255 Date 0 0,First,#,"Spaces",DocUpdate,0,255;'

#Load from floorplan layers created using Extract BIM File Floorplan tool
load_floorplan_layers="LOAD_FROM_FLOORPLAN_LAYERS"
in_floorplan_footprint='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Floorplan_Dataset/Footprint'
in_floorplan_polygon='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Floorplan_Dataset/planPolygon'
in_floorplan_polyline='C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Floorplan_Dataset/planPolyline'
load_roof_layers="LOAD_ROOFS"

#Run the tool
arcpy.indoors.ImportIFCToIndoorDataset_Indoors(in_bim_file_workspace, target_facility_features, facility_name,
                                                target_level_features, target_unit_features, target_detail_features,
                                                ground_floor_name, unit_properties_mapping, load_floorplan_layers,
                                                in_floorplan_footprint, in_floorplan_polygon, in_floorplan_polyline,
                                                target_unit3d_features, target_detail3d_features, target_facility3d_features,
                                                load_roof_layers, ground_elevation_zero)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-ifc-to-indoor-dataset.html#derived-output

## 將 IFC 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**english**　Import IFC To Indoor Dataset

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-ifc-to-indoor-dataset.html

**id**　619f58f00142de75bf-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-ifc-to-indoor-dataset.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Import IFC To Indoor Dataset，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入 BIM 文件工作空間 [in_bim_file_workspace]、目標設施點圖徵 [target_facility_features]、設施點名稱 [facility_name]、目標樓層圖徵 [target_level_features]、目標單元圖徵 [target_unit_features]、目標細節圖徵 [target_detail_features]、一樓名稱 [ground_floor_name]、單元屬性映射 （可選） [unit_properties_mapping]、從樓層平面圖圖層加載 （可選） [load_floorplan_layers]、輸入樓層平面圖覆蓋區圖層 （可選） [in_floorplan_footprint]、輸入樓層平面圖面圖層 （可選） [in_floorplan_polygon]、輸入樓層平面圖折線圖層 （可選） [in_floorplan_polyline]、目標單元 3D 圖徵 （可選） [target_unit3d_features]、目標細節 3D 圖徵 （可選） [target_detail3d_features]、目標設施點 3D 圖徵 （可選） [target_facility3d_features]、加載屋頂 （可選） [load_roofs]、將一樓高程設置為零 [ground_elevation_zero]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-ifc-to-indoor-dataset.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_bim_file_workspace", "label_zh": "輸入 BIM 文件工作空間", "label_en": "Input BIM File Workspace", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "BIM File Workspace", "description_en": "The input IFC workspace."}, {"parameter": "target_facility_features", "label_zh": "目標設施點圖徵", "label_en": "Target Facility Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Facilities feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model and resides in the same workspace as the target Levels, Units, and Details features."}, {"parameter": "facility_name", "label_zh": "設施點名稱", "label_en": "Facility Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The common name of the building. If a feature with the same name exists in the target Facilities layer, it will be updated, along with all of the associated Levels, Units, and Details features."}, {"parameter": "target_level_features", "label_zh": "目標樓層圖徵", "label_en": "Target Level Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Levels feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model and resides in the same workspace as the target Facilities, Units, and Details features."}, {"parameter": "target_unit_features", "label_zh": "目標單元圖徵", "label_en": "Target Unit Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Units feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model and resides in the same workspace as the target Facilities, Levels, and Details features."}, {"parameter": "target_detail_features", "label_zh": "目標細節圖徵", "label_en": "Target Detail Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Details feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model and resides in the same workspace as the target Facilities, Levels, and Units features."}, {"parameter": "ground_floor_name", "label_zh": "一樓名稱", "label_en": "Ground Floor Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The ground floor of the building. The vertical order of the levels is derived from this value. Any levels with an elevation that is less than the specified ground floor will be assigned a negative vertical order."}, {"parameter": "unit_properties_mapping", "label_zh": "單元屬性映射 （可選）", "label_en": "Unit Properties Mapping (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field Mappings", "description_en": "Controls which attribute fields in the Units layer will be populated with field values from the input IFC Spaces layer. The fields must exist before running the tool. It is recommended that you map fields from the input IFC Spaces layer to fields from the Units layer that have the same field type."}, {"parameter": "load_floorplan_layers", "label_zh": "從樓層平面圖圖層加載 （可選）", "label_en": "Load From Floorplan Layers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether features will be loaded from input floor plan layers created by the Extract BIM File Floorplan tool. Checked—Features will be loaded from the input floor plan layers created by the Extract BIM File Floorplan tool. Any selections set on the input layers will be honored. Unchecked—Features will be loaded from the .ifc file. All levels will be loaded and no selections will be honored. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "in_floorplan_footprint", "label_zh": "輸入樓層平面圖覆蓋區圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Floorplan Footprint Layer (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The Floorplan Footprint feature layer created using the Extract BIM File Floorplan tool. Features in this layer will be used to create features in the target Facilities layer."}, {"parameter": "in_floorplan_polygon", "label_zh": "輸入樓層平面圖面圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Floorplan Polygon Layer (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The Floorplan Polygon feature layer created using the Extract BIM File Floorplan tool. Features in this layer will be used to create features in the target Levels and Units layers."}, {"parameter": "in_floorplan_polyline", "label_zh": "輸入樓層平面圖折線圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Floorplan Polyline Layer (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The Floorplan Polyline feature layer created using the Extract BIM File Floorplan tool. Features in this layer will be used to create features in the target Details layer."}, {"parameter": "target_unit3d_features", "label_zh": "目標單元 3D 圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Target Unit 3D Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Units3D feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model. Multipatch unit features will be created in the target Units3D layer from the Spaces category of the input .ifc file."}, {"parameter": "target_detail3d_features", "label_zh": "目標細節 3D 圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Target Detail 3D Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Details3D feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model. Multipatch detail features will be created in the target Details3D layer from the following categories in the input .ifc file: Doors, Columns, Walls, Ramps, Stairs, Windows, Curtain Walls, and Structural Columns."}, {"parameter": "target_facility3d_features", "label_zh": "目標設施點 3D 圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Target Facility 3D Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Facilities3D feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model. A multipatch facility feature will be created in the target Facilities3D layer from the ExteriorShell category in the input .ifc file."}, {"parameter": "load_roofs", "label_zh": "加載屋頂 （可選）", "label_en": "Load Roofs (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether roof features will be imported as levels features. Checked—Roof elements will be imported from the input .ifc file and used along with floors features to create levels features in the Indoors workspace. Unchecked—Roof features will not be imported from the input .ifc file. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "ground_elevation_zero", "label_zh": "將一樓高程設置為零", "label_en": "Set Ground Floor Elevation To Zero (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the elevation of the ground floor will be set to 0 or the elevation of the floors features in the georeferenced BIM model will be used. Checked—The ground floor elevation will be set to 0, and the z-values of other levels will be defined based on their relationship to the ground floor. This is the default. Unchecked—The elevation of level features will match the elevation of the floors in the georeferenced BIM model."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.indoors.ImportIFCToIndoorDataset(in_bim_file_workspace, target_facility_features, facility_name, target_level_features, target_unit_features, target_detail_features, ground_floor_name, {unit_properties_mapping}, {load_floorplan_layers}, {in_floorplan_footprint}, {in_floorplan_polygon}, {in_floorplan_polyline}, {target_unit3d_features}, {target_detail3d_features}, {target_facility3d_features}, {load_roofs}, {ground_elevation_zero})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_bim_file_workspace、target_facility_features、facility_name、target_level_features、target_unit_features、target_detail_features、ground_floor_name、unit_properties_mapping、load_floorplan_layers、in_floorplan_footprint、in_floorplan_polygon、in_floorplan_polyline、target_unit3d_features、target_detail3d_features、target_facility3d_features、load_roofs、ground_elevation_zero。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 導入關聯 (公共設施網絡工具) · 用法

**id**　61a6bf8518a73e9cc2-2

**english**　Import Associations

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/import-associations.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/import-associations.html

**text**　關聯必須位于單個 .csv 文件中。
公共設施網絡中必須存在 .csv 文件中列出的圖徵。
.csv 文件中列出的圖徵必須具有支持的規則。
在使用企業級地理資料庫時，輸入公共設施網絡參數值必須來自以資料庫公共設施網絡所有者 身份建立的資料庫連接。
.csv 文件中的信息按以下格式存儲：ASSOCIATIONTYPE、FROMFEATURECLASS、FROMASSETGROUP、FROMASSETTYPE、FROMGLOBALID、FROMTERMINAL、TOFEATURECLASS、TOASSETGROUP、TOASSETTYPE、TOGLOBALID、TOTERMINAL、ISCONTENTVISIBLE、PERCENTALONG。 當輸入公共設施網絡參數引用包含電信域網絡 的公共設施網絡時，.csv 文件需要另外 4 列：FROMFIRSTUNIT、FROMLASTUNIT、TOFIRSTUNIT 和 TOLASTUNIT。 當存在電信域網絡時，即使目標域網絡為傳統域網絡，這些標頭欄位也為必需項。 有關詳細信息，請參閱關聯表 幫助主題。 .csv 列 列 描述 示例 ASSOCIATIONTYPE 關聯的類型。 交匯點交匯點連通性 交匯點邊自連通性 交匯點邊中跨連通性 交匯點邊至連通性 包含 結構附件 包含 FROMFEATURECLASS / TOFEATURECLASS 參與關聯的圖徵類或對象。 ElectricDistributionDevice、ElectricDistributionAssembly、StructureJunction FROMASSETGROUP / TOASSETGROUP 圖徵的資產組。 Transformer、Fuse、Elbow FROMASSETTYPE / TOASSETTYPE 圖徵的資產類型。 Streetlight、Attachment、Single-phase padmounted FROMGLOBALID / TOGLOBALID 圖徵的全局 ID。 {16558C73-013C-4169-A095-7E9DCD57EB57} FROMTERMINAL / TOTERMINAL 參與關聯的終端。 這僅適用于交匯點-交匯點連通性關聯。 單一終端、負載、來源 ISCONTENTVISIBLE 布爾型標記指明內容是否可見。 這僅適用于包含關聯。 False、True PERCENTALONG 交匯點對象和邊對象之間交匯點邊中跨連通性關聯的延伸百分比值。 0.75 FROMFIRSTUNIT / TOFIRSTUNIT “自”側或“至”側分組的第一個單元 ID。 僅當輸入公共設施網絡參數引用包含電信域網絡的公共設施網絡時才需要此項。 1 FROMLASTUNIT / TOLASTUNIT “自”側或“至”側分組的最后一個單元 ID。 與“自”和“至”第一個單元屬性配合使用，以確定“自”側或“至”側上連接的連續組的大小。 僅當輸入公共設施網絡參數引用包含電信域網絡的公共設施網絡時才需要此項。 12

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/import-associations.html#usage

- 指定要導入的關聯類型。 ALL—將導入所有關聯類型。 JUNCTION_JUNCTION_CONNECTIVITY—將導入交匯點-交匯點連通性關聯類型。 CONTAINMENT—將導入包含關聯類型。 STRUCTURAL_ATTACHMENT—將導入結構附件關聯類型。 JUNCTION_EDGE_FROM_CONNECTIVITY—將導入交匯點-邊連通性（自邊的一側）關聯類型。 JUNCTION_EDGE_MIDSPAN_CONNECTIVITY—將導入交匯點-邊連通性（中跨）關聯類型。 JUNCTION_EDGE_TO_CONNECTIVITY—將導入交匯點-邊連通性（至邊的一側）關聯類型。
- String

## 導入關聯 (公共設施網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**english**　Import Associations

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/import-associations.html

**id**　61a6bf8518a73e9cc2-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/import-associations.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Import Associations，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入公共設施網絡 [in_utility_network]、關聯類型 [association_type]、輸入文件 [csv_file]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/import-associations.html#parameters

## 181000：<Value> 參數值：<value> 無效。 輸入圖層必須具有名為 LEVEL_ID 的欄位或作為樓層感知型地圖圖層。 · 描述

**id**　61a7855fcc8b5d93b3-1

**english**　181000: <Value> parameter value: <value> is invalid. Input layer must have a field named LEVEL_ID or be a floor-aware map layer.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 180901-181000

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/180001-190000/tool-errors-and-warnings-180976-181000-181000.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/180001-190000/tool-errors-and-warnings-180976-181000-181000.html

**text**　工具需要識別指定參數輸入中的圖徵所在的樓層。 為此，輸入圖層必須具有名為 LEVEL_ID 的欄位或作為樓層感知型地圖圖層。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/180001-190000/tool-errors-and-warnings-180976-181000-181000.html#description
